# Verification passes

The verifiers' passes, ship steps and production tables of every round from the focus round to the realtime round, one file per pass since Round 1 (`docs/NEXT.md` 5.2 item 3). Each file holds the text of its pass unchanged. A citation such as "VERIFICATION C2-F29" or "VERIFICATION.md, Polish round, the production table" names a pass by a heading below. A new pass is a new file in its round's folder under `verification/`, and the run files of a round go into the same folder (`docs/gslides-parity/README.md`, rule 7).

# Round 1, pass 1 (the verifier, 2026-10-01)

The verifier's first pass on Round 1 of the next program (`docs/NEXT.md` 3.2 and 4.1): the hotfixes H2 to H10 and the 21 pushes of 4.1.6, in the worktree `/Users/kevinliu/repos/Turboslide-next` on `next/round1` at `d7ac6431` (58 commits over the merge `f3e9cd86`, the tree clean at the start). The pass ran on 2026-10-03 and 2026-10-04 from 22:10Z (every time below is UTC). Read first: `docs/NEXT.md` 3, 4.0, 4.1, 5, 6 and 7; `docs/FOCUS.md` 6.2; the eleven notes under `docs/gslides-parity/round1/build/`; `git log f3e9cd86..HEAD`. No claim of the integrator's note was taken as read. The chain was run again step by step on the tree. The integrator's ledgers were read by id. The round's rows were run again on this pass's own node-server pair and on the integrator's preview. Every surface of the divergence map and every clutter cut was driven by a script at a person's pace in both appearances at 1440 and 390 on both bases, and the pictures were looked at beside the audit's. The base tree `f3e9cd86` was served from a scratch export to read check step 21 on it. Ports: 4521 and 4522 for this pass, and 4321 for the chain's own servers. No deployment was made and nothing was pushed. Other projects' self tests and the realtime round's fix lanes shared the machine: the one minute load read 8 to 70 over the pass, and it is written beside every run.

The working files are in the session scratch folder `r1v1/` (the scripts `chain/run.sh`, `servers.sh`, `gate.sh`, `hand.mjs`, `cuts.mjs`, `move.mjs`, `signin.mjs`, `base21.sh`, and the raw pictures). The ledgers and the 77 pictures that this section names are under `docs/gslides-parity/round1/verification/pass1/` (WebP, 1.49 MB in all, the largest 88 KB; the round's tracked pictures go from 21.26 MB to 22.75 MB, under NEXT.md 5.3's 25 MB).

## V.1 Verdict

**Round 1's own work is done as NEXT.md 4.1 and 3.2 write it. The branch is not ready to ship as it stands, for three reasons. One is Round 1's. One predates the round. The third is a driver that reads red in this branch's whole matrix runs.**

- Round 1's work. Every one of the 33 rows the round entered passed on this pass's local run on the node-server build of `d7ac6431`. Every hosted one passed on the preview, where the integrator's 28 hosted rows read 28 of 28 (V.4). Every surface of the divergence map that the round owns reads closed in the pictures, at both widths, in both appearances, on both bases (V.6). Rank 24 is H11's and Round 3's. Every clutter cut was used through its new home, and every word of item 21 reads as written except two: the animated pattern's own chip still reads "Shader", and another person's label is not "Guest <label>" (finding 7, both recorded as the integrator's deviations). Kevin's "move around ANYTHING" holds: all eleven object kinds of the default Insert menu were resized by a corner or end handle with the opposite corner held, and moved by the drag to the sheet pixel, on the preview and on 4521, and the PDF and the PowerPoint of those slides carry each object where it was left (V.6). Sign in and sign out work on the local accounts server: the code arrives, Sign out takes one click and leaves a new anonymous cookie, and Sign In comes back.
- Round 1's cause. H2 scoped the Import slides picker to the browser's own and shared decks (an anonymous browser's Recent record), so a fresh browser is no longer offered the deployment's GT brand deck, and the earlier round's core row `slides.import.none-preselected` (feature `slides`, which cannot be parked) reads red (finding 2). Either the picker offers the example deck, or the row and its driver follow a product decision. That decision is Kevin's question in V.10. On the preview the row stops earlier: its driver's copy of the brand deck is never opened in the browser, so the picker, which reads an anonymous browser's Recent record since H2, never offers it.
- Not Round 1's. The earlier rounds' rows that read red on the memory tier before this round are still red on this branch: the undo class of eight arrange rows and five images rows (the realtime round's R2-F1, fixed by `f8ec63ff` on `realtime/round`, which this branch does not carry), ten more rows and four not driven (finding 1). The ship rebases onto the realtime round's final `main` anyway (NEXT.md 6.1 rule 3), and these rows are read again on the rebased tree before the first push.
- The driver. `present.laser.visible` is red in both whole matrix runs on this branch and green on the realtime round's run of record and in both narrowed reruns. Its driver reads the laser where the spec's earlier tests left the title's ink, and the product draws the dot and its ring there (finding 3). Why the realtime round's whole run did not meet the same ink was not traced. The row's feature cannot be parked, so the row holds the ship until its driver reads the dot on an empty ground.

The two link rows the integrator read red on the node-server build passed on the preview and in this pass's check step 32 (finding 6, a flake). The 19 `gestures.*` rows the node-server build never drove were driven in check step 32 and passed (finding 17).

The integrator's open questions are answered as follows:
- Check step 21: all 23 failures also fail on the base tree `f3e9cd86` with the same words. Round 1 causes none of them, including the eight the integrator's note attributed to B3b#9, B3a#7 and B3b#14 (finding 12). The base tree fails five `text-editing` tests more than HEAD does.
- Check step 32, which the integrator left to the ship, ran from 05:13Z to 08:44Z (load 23.24 at the start and 24.23 at the end). It read 1,101 rows: 930 passed, 80 failed and 91 not driven, with retries zero and one cost row over its ceiling on the dev server (finding 21). Its unparkable reds are the standing rows of finding 1, `present.laser.visible` (finding 3), the two H2 rows on the file store (finding 19), the name prompt race (finding 20), Kevin's standing `sync.title.concurrent-both-kept`, and the measurement row `decks.home.load-budget` (ready 608 ms against 500 on a dev server).

The severity 2 list is two items: the trash page lists the whole store for every visitor (finding 4), and the GT template's seven speed mark slides move their drawing over their text on a seller's first canvas write (finding 5).

## V.2 The gates on the tree

`scripts/check.mjs` stops at its first red step, so the chain was run one step at a time in order (`r1v1/chain/run.sh`: each step `node scripts/check.mjs --only <n>`, a browser step started only at a one minute load under 24 after five minute waits, the steps that drive the chain's server under `.turboslide/e2e.lock`), with the times and loads in `r1v1/chain/ledger.tsv`. Step 7 imports the live Prototemplate deck into `decks/gt-brand` in place, so steps 8 to 26 read the 95 slide import, as a full chain does. The import was put back from git before steps 27 to 31 (the untracked slides it adds moved to the scratch folder, not deleted), so the node-server build of step 31, which this pass's local runs serve, carries the committed seed deck. Step 20 rewrites `docs/gslides-parity/verification-3/parity-audit.json`; its output was kept in the scratch folder and the tracked file restored at once.

## V.3 The run ledger

Every run this pass started, against the preview and against its own servers, with its start and end, the load at both, the commit and the ledger. The preview serves `c17fd8bf` (the hosted smoke's build commit row); the commits after it change drivers, the smoke, tests and one product file, `decks.trash.tsx` (`71e42ab4`), so the preview runs read the round's product at `c17fd8bf` with the drivers of `d7ac6431`. The local runs serve the node-server build check step 31 wrote at `d7ac6431` (copied to the scratch folder before the runs), on the pair 4521 (`TURBOSLIDE_MAIL=capture`) and 4522 (`TURBOSLIDE_MAIL=off`), each with its own overlay on the tmp store, the memory tier, its own identity database at the overlay's path, the local open surface, the fake Google pair, and the assist and logo fixtures (`r1v1/servers.sh`, the integrator's recipe on this pass's ports), restarted fresh before the accounts run and before the round's rows (the library's limit of 10 sign in mails per IP per hour holds on a tmp store, finding 14).

## V.5 Findings

Severity follows the round's prompt. Severity 3 is a row broken, a feature that does not do what NEXT.md says, or an earlier round's core row red; Kevin's standing rows `sync.title.concurrent-both-kept` and `decks.file.open-list-search` are excepted. Severity 2 is a defect a seller meets. Severity 1 is cosmetic or a recorded flake. Each finding names one owner. The whole matrix comes from the integrator's run of record (`round1-local-memory.json`, 1,101 rows), read by id against the realtime round's run of record (`realtime-local-memory.json`). It lists 61 rows of features that cannot be parked as failed or not driven. Eleven of them read green on the realtime round's run, or are the round's own new row, and each was read again: `people.chip-tooltip-trust`, `people.comment-departed-guest`, `realtime.departed-guest.name-stable` and `share.dialog.you-label` (the drivers named the guest through the first Share's prompt, which B3b#13 removed; `c206823c` and `1c821bbf`; passed in the integrator's narrowed rerun, 7 of 7), `present.laser.visible` (passed narrowed, and red again in this pass's step 32, finding 3), `chrome.buttons.one-rule` (its driver's refused address, `addedf48`; passed on this pass's local run), `decks.trash.restore` and `decks.trash.lists-after-restore` (H2's restore, `71e42ab4`; passed on the integrator's dev server reading), and the three rows of findings 2 and 6. The other 50 read the same on both runs: the 27 rows of finding 1, the 19 `gestures.*` rows and three template rows of finding 17, and the parked row `arrange.group.tail-text-controls`. This pass's check step 32 (V.2) is a second whole matrix run, on the chain's dev server with the file store, and it is read row by row in findings 1, 3, 17, 19, 20 and 21.

**Severity 3**

1. **The earlier rounds' core rows that were red before this round are still red (integrator).** These rows are red on this branch and on the realtime round's run of record alike, with the same readings:
   - The undo class on the memory tier: `arrange.group.chords`, `arrange.group.menu-regroup`, `arrange.distribute.horizontal`, `arrange.distribute.vertical`, `arrange.snap.guides-on-off`, `arrange.clipboard.menu-copy-paste`, `arrange.redo.after-undone-duplicate`, `arrange.context.rotate-distribute`, `images.resize.eight-handles`, `images.resize.eight-handles-shift`, `images.resize.alt-centre`, `images.rotate.ring` and `images.crop.redo`. The walk reads every box landing where the drag put it ("351,210 300x200 ... readout during 300 × 200") and then "undo restored false" (`.turboslide/round1/int/local-memory/core-walk.json`). This pass's own drags agree that the gestures themselves land (V.6).
   - `versions.show-changes-marks`, `images.border.drawn`, `images.insert.no-external-banner`, `text.paragraph.toolbar-live`, `text.list.enter-tab-no-error`, `text.size.run-and-typed-value`, `help.check-slides.plain-sentence`, `export.refusal.sentence-and-retry`, `export.picture.progress-and-capture` and `shaders.export.missing-frame-row`.
   - Not driven: `sync.resend.idempotent`, `decks.recent.this-browser-sentence`, `realtime.agent.write-announced` and `text.clipboard.paste-without-formatting`.
   
   `b3b.md` read the eight arrange rows red on `main`'s tree as well. `f8ec63ff` on `realtime/round` makes undo and redo wait for each step's admission, and it is the fix for the undo class. The ship rebases onto the realtime round's final `main`, and these rows are read on the rebased tree before the first push. This pass's check step 32 on the chain's dev server read 25 of these 27 rows the same way. `images.border.drawn` passed there, and `realtime.agent.write-announced` was driven and passed. It also read `versions.undo-restore` red ("Cmd+Z brought the current version back false", the same undo class; green on the two node-server runs), and `text.select.shift-home-line` and `realtime.title.two-typers` red. Both of those read both ways across the three runs, and the realtime round records the second as flaky.
2. **`slides.import.none-preselected` is red because of H2 (HA).** Since `6ac04388` the Import slides picker lists the browser's own and shared decks (an anonymous browser's Recent record), so a fresh browser is no longer offered the seed GT brand deck. The walk then copies the GT template to import from. On the node-server build that copy is refused (finding 17). On the integrator's vite dev reading the copy opened with its 95 tiles and the selection read right, but the three picked slides did not land ("landed 0") while the worker rendered the copy's tiles (`round1-local-rerun-dev-slides.json`). This pass ran the slides walk area on the preview (05:14:31Z to 05:21:59Z, load 14.14 to 25.80, `pass1/v1-preview-slides.json`): 83 rows, 82 passed. `decks.file.import-slides-deck` passed: its driver opens its setup copy in the browser since `df4f8ede`, so the picker, which reads an anonymous browser's Recent record since H2, offers it after 1,258 ms and the two slides land. `slides.import.none-preselected` failed, and its driver does not open its copy: "the copy import-source-mutdgnj6 was not offered by the Import slides picker within 65 s". On a hosted tier the row therefore stops at the picker, before the selection the integrator read on vite dev. The row passed on the realtime round's run of record. Either the picker offers the deployment's example deck to every browser, or the row's words and its driver follow Kevin's answer (V.10 question 1).
3. **`present.laser.visible` is red in both whole matrix runs on this branch (integrator).** The integrator's run of record and this pass's check step 32 both read "the drawn diameter is 14 px with its ring" as 1, with the core, the ring and the ground all `#101010`. The row passed in both narrowed reruns. The realtime round's run of record passed it. The trace of step 32 (`.turboslide/core-gate/playwright/core-present-present-laser-*/trace.zip`) shows why: the spec's deck carries its earlier tests' title "Pipeline review: Acme, Q3 2026", and the show draws it at the full width. The laser dot at the driver's point (700, 440) sits on the title's ink, so the pixel read meets no ground and stops at the centre. In the frames the dot and its white ring are drawn over the letters. The product draws the laser, and the driver measures it on a dark ground. A narrowed run has a fresh deck with no title text there. The row's feature `present` cannot be parked, so the driver must read the dot where the slide is empty, or measure the ring against the ink.

**Severity 2**

4. **The trash page lists the whole store for every visitor (integrator, `df4f8ede`).** On the preview `/decks/trash` answered in 13.5 to 14.7 s cold: the hosted smoke's row read 14,697 and 13,472 ms in the integrator's two runs and 14,120 ms in this pass's run at 22:22Z. Warm, it answered in 2.1 to 2.5 s of server time over three readings at about 22:25Z, and its `server-timing` names no store read. `/decks` answers in 4 ms. `listTrashedDecks` (`apps/studio/src/server/decks.ts` 352) calls `trashScope()`, which asks `listingScope` with `page: false`. The anonymous principal minted for a cookieless stranger therefore reads as `own`, and the server lists every head of the preview's 172 decks and reads the access record of every trashed one before it keeps none. Before the round the route listed the store the same way and read no record. H2 took the listing off `/decks`, and NEXT.md 3.2 counts "about 150 Blob heads per anonymous view to 0"; the trash page still makes that listing for a visitor who owns nothing. An anonymous visitor's trash can be read from this browser's Recent record, the way `/decks` reads its list. Round 2's index object (NEXT.md 4.2.2 fix 1) replaces the listing for every caller.
5. **The GT template's speed mark slides move their drawing over their text on a seller's first canvas write (B4).** In check step 24 the seven speed mark slides of the committed GT template, and of the imported `gt-brand`, convert to a canvas 3.91 to 18.58 percent off their grammar render, in both appearances. The picture `chain-step24-speed-plate` shows the converted plate drawn up over the two body columns. A deck made from the GT template since B4b#6 carries these seven slides among its 95, and the first drag on any object of one of them converts the slide. NEXT.md 4.1.3 item 24 expected the difference, and B4's request 17 holds it open together with request 6 (the importer's inline `<svg>`). It is still a defect a seller meets on the template this round ships.

The guard runs the smoke first on its fresh preview, and production answered the cookieless request 302 `MISS` (thumbnails are open there), so the guard is not expected to read it red. The gestures setup's "slide null" on the node build was not traced.
18. **Check step 31 keeps its standing reds (integrator).** 102 of 148 budgets were met. The 46 red are the 32 JS size rows (the largest chunk 1,157 KB against 586 KB on every route), 5 DOM counts (`/deck/gt-brand` 2,511, `/edit/gt-brand` 2,172, the filmstrip of 85 cards 2,703, each against 1,500) and 9 timings read at a load of 20.86 to 23.10, which NEXT.md 4.0 makes void. The person's user agent of H8 holds ("contexts open as Mozilla/5.0 ... Chrome/147").
19. **`decks.list.own-and-shared` and `decks.list.action-scoped` read red on the chain's file store (HA).** In check step 32 a fresh browser listed six decks and the principal's `deck.list` held three more than its own. On a checkout's file store H2 lists the whole store to every caller by design (ha.md). These rows should skip there, as the integrator's note asks of `accounts.decks-list-scoped` in check step 26. They passed on the tmp store pair and on the preview.
20. **`share.name-prompt.empty-field` timed out in check step 32 (B3b).** The trace shows the race. The deck's first write raised the title row's name plate while the spec's setup was still settling, and the setup's own step closed it (`dialog.namePrompt.close`). The row then waited for a plate that had already gone, and next for a Share dialog it never opened, until the 150 s limit. It passed on this pass's local run and on the integrator's runs. The driver reads its own setup's plate and is a recorded flake.
21. **`cost.editor-editing.calls` read 76.66 function requests a minute against its ceiling of 75 in check step 32 (integrator).** The reading was 230 in 3 min on the chain's vite dev server and file store. The integrator's node-server run read 70.98. The row's ceiling holds the ship only on the preview (NEXT.md 6.1 rule 4), and the round's preview runs did not include the cost probe. This pass records the local reading.

## V.6 Hand drives

Four scripts in the session scratch (`r1v1/hand.mjs`, `cuts.mjs`, `move.mjs`, `signin.mjs`) drive the product through its controls at a person's pace (Playwright with a 40 to 60 ms pause on every action, a 0.5 to 2.5 s wait after every open), log the rows, labels and tooltips the eye reads beside each picture, and refuse to start at a one minute load of 24 or more. Each made its own deck from `/new` by a first write and removed it by its id at the end (trash, then remove, then a 404); on the preview each id was read again with the bearer after the last run and answered `deck.info` 404.

# Round 1, pass 2 (the verifier, 2026-10-01)

This is the verifier's second pass on Round 1, after the fix round. The worktree is `/Users/kevinliu/repos/Turboslide-next` on `next/round1` at `fd81dcc6`, and the tree was clean at the start. Pass 1 read `d7ac6431` and wrote `8154b6f4`. The fix round added eight commits after that: `58f82a30` (H6 fix), `96432f76`, `ffd70351` and `026db8e4` (H2 fix), `7f340372` and `85f002b1` (B4b#6 fix), `acf910e7` (B3a#8 fix), and the integrator's note `fd81dcc6`. The pass ran on 2026-10-04 from 11:11Z to 14:30Z (every time below is UTC). Read first: `docs/NEXT.md` 4.0, 4.1 and 6; `docs/FOCUS.md` 6.2; every note under `docs/gslides-parity/round1/build/`, with the "Round 1 fix round" sections of `integrator.md`, `ha.md` and `b4.md` read in full; `git log 8154b6f4..HEAD` and each fix commit's diff. No claim of a fixer was taken as read. This pass measures the fixes. The chain was run again step by step. The integrator's fix ledgers were read by id. Every row the round entered and every row a fix touched was run again on this pass's own servers, and each fix was driven by hand. Ports: 4521 and 4522, and 4321 for the chain's own servers. No deployment was made and nothing was pushed. Nothing was created or changed on Vercel, Cloudflare, GitHub or Google. The one minute load read 6 to 56 over the pass, and it is written beside every run.

The preview `turboslide-lp2z0cdl8` serves `c17fd8bf`. Server source files changed after that commit: `ffd70351` changes `apps/studio/src/server/decks.ts`, `deck-scope.ts` and three store backends, `026db8e4` changes the Import slides dialog, and `7f340372` changes the GT template that the server copies. The preview therefore cannot read any fix, so this pass did not drive it again. Its one use was the by-id check of the scratch decks (V2.3). Every hosted reading of a fix waits for the ship's preview (V2.8).

The working files are in the session scratch folder `r1v2/`. The scripts are `chain/run.sh`, `seq.sh`, `after.sh`, `extra.sh`, `extra2.sh`, `servers.sh`, `dev.sh`, `gate.sh`, `gate-dev.sh`, `drive.sh`, `trash.mjs`, `importdlg.mjs`, `speed.mjs` and `typed.sh`, with pass 1's `hand.mjs`, `cuts.mjs`, `move.mjs` and `signin.mjs`. The raw pictures are there too. The ledgers and the 24 pictures this section names are under `docs/gslides-parity/round1/verification/pass2/` (WebP, 0.68 MB in all, the largest 102 KB). The round's tracked pictures go from 23.67 MB to 24.35 MB, under NEXT.md 5.3's 25 MB.

## V2.1 Verdict

**The fix round's work holds on this pass's servers. Findings 2, 3, 4 and 5 read closed, and no severity 3 or 2 finding is open against Round 1's own work. The branch is not ready to push, for three reasons that belong to the ship step.**

- The fixes. `slides.import.none-preselected` passed in the slides walk area on the dev server: "landed 3 1674 ms after Import" over the copy's 95 tiles, with at most 6 tile pictures in flight (finding 2). `present.laser.visible` passed in the whole present spec on the node-server build, after the tests that write the title (finding 3). The trash page answers a cookieless stranger in 3 to 21 ms with no store read. A browser's own trashed deck is in the page's first HTML, and another browser's id forged into the cookie lists nothing. Every trash row passed (finding 4). The GT template's seven speed mark slides convert to a canvas within 0.021 percent of their drawing. Each drawing moves by a drag and leaves the text where it was (finding 5).
- No regression. The round's 33 rows passed again: 31 spec rows in one run and the two hotfix accounts rows narrowed. The divergence map and the clutter cuts read as in pass 1 at 1440 and 390 in both appearances. All eleven object kinds of the default Insert menu were resized and moved to the sheet pixel, and the PDF and the PowerPoint carry them where they were left. Sign in and sign out work on the local accounts server. The chain's red steps read as pass 1 read them: 15, 18, 20, 21 and 26 with the same failures, and 31 with its standing budgets. Step 24 went from 28 pairs over budget to 14, and those 14 are the chain's import of `decks/gt-brand` (b4.md request 22, Round 2).
- The ship step's reasons. (1) Finding 1 is not fixed on the branch. The earlier rounds' undo class and the other standing rows close only on a tree that carries the realtime round's controller. `origin/main` now carries that undo fix as `277554da`. The rows are read on the ship's rebased tree before the first push. (2) The hosted halves of findings 2 and 4 wait for a preview of these commits: the import on the blob tier, where HA read a Blob concurrency refusal at `c17fd8bf`, and the trash page's time on a store of the preview's size. (3) The realtime round's ship stopped. `realtime/round`'s `5f9222d0` records that `collab.presence.join-within-2s` read red six times of seven on production at `7d5f961c`, and that production was rolled back by hand to `5ce68a72` at 12:11:54Z. NEXT.md 6.1 rule 3 holds Round 1's first push until that round's fix forward and its production table. `origin/main` at `7d5f961c` also carries H3 (`f2e648f1`) and H6 (`a73db740`). The 67 pick order applies clean onto `f3e9cd86`, and onto `origin/main` its first pick already conflicts (V2.2).

Pass 2 adds three severity 1 findings: the Import slides tiles at the end of the list wait behind every tile scrolled past (P2-1, integrator), the trash page draws boxed cards (P2-2, B2), and a store test pins the seed deck at 85 slides, so check step 5 reads red while the chain's import is in the tree (P2-3, B4).

## V2.2 The gates on the tree

The chain was run one step at a time with `r1v2/chain/run.sh`. A browser step started only at a one minute load under 24, after five minute waits. A step that serves on 4321 also waited until the port was free, because another checkout's chain uses it. The steps that drive the chain's server ran under `.turboslide/e2e.lock`. The times and loads are in `pass2/chain-ledger.tsv`.

The order was steps 1 to 16, 19 and 22 to 25 with step 7's import in place. The import was then put back from git, and steps 27 to 31 and 33 ran, so the node-server build carries the committed seed deck. Step 7 then ran again, followed by 17, 18, 20, 21 and 26. The import's untracked slides were moved to the scratch folder each time it was put back. ## V2.6 Hand drives

Each drive went through the product's own controls at a person's pace: Playwright with a 40 to 60 ms pause on every action and a 0.5 to 2.5 s wait after every open. Each drive logs the facts beside its pictures and refuses to start at a one minute load of 24 or more. The pictures were looked at in grids beside pass 1's and the audit's (`next/brand-surfaces/`).

### The trash page (finding 4)

`trash.mjs` on the node-server build (12:02:59Z to 12:04:04Z, load 14.23 to 27.12):
- A cookieless GET of `/decks/trash`, three times: 200 in 21, 4 and 3 ms. The first HTML holds the empty state and no card, and `server-timing` names no trash read.
- Browser A: `/decks/trash` is empty. A deck made from `/new` by its first write was moved to the trash with File > Move to trash. `/decks` showed "Moved to trash" with Undo. The trash record held the id in `turboslide:in-trash` and in the `ts-trash` cookie.
- `/decks/trash` listed the card 110 ms after the click, and the card was already in the first HTML. The document's `server-timing` read `trash-ids;dur=0.1`. The card reads "Untitled presentation Trashed 5:03 AM · 2 slides" with Restore and Delete forever. At 1440 and 390 in both appearances, the card is in the first HTML and the page does not scroll sideways (`local-trash-listed-1440-light`, `local-trash-listed-390-dark`).
- Browser B, fresh: "Trash is empty" with the figure and Empty trash disabled (`local-trash-stranger-1440-light`). With A's id forged into B's `ts-trash` cookie and its storage, the page listed nothing, put nothing in its first HTML, and cleared the forged id from B's storage.
- A clicked Restore. The snackbar read "Restored Untitled presentation", the card left, the record emptied, and `/decks` listed the deck again. The row menu's Move to trash put it back in the trash.
- Delete forever opened "Delete Untitled presentation forever? This cannot be undone. 2 slides and every version of Untitled presentation are deleted." with Cancel and Delete forever (`local-trash-confirm-1440-light`). Cancel kept the card. Delete forever removed it, the editor answered 404, and the record and the cookie emptied.

New in pass 2:

P2-1. **The Import slides tiles at the end of a long list wait behind every tile scrolled past (integrator, severity 1).** `026db8e4` puts each tile in the picture gate's queue when it first comes within 200 px of the list's visible area. A tile that scrolls away keeps its place, because the observer disconnects after the first sighting and the cancel runs only when the tile unmounts. A person who scrolls to the end of the copy's 95 tiles therefore sees the tiles in view stay blank until every tile above them has loaded. On the dev server at a load of 6.7, the 8 tiles in view were blank 20 s after the scroll, and the gate was at tile 29 (`dev-import-scrolled-20s-1440-light`). Their titles are drawn and the import itself works: 3 slides landed in 609 ms. The gate's limit and its low priority are what close finding 2. A tile that leaves the visible area could give its queue place back, or the waiting tiles could start nearest the visible area first. `ImportSlides.tsx` has no Round 1 owner, and `026db8e4` is the integrator's.

P2-2. **The trash page draws its decks as boxed cards (B2, severity 1).** `/decks` lists ruled rows since B2b#16. `/decks/trash`, one click away, still draws each deck as a framed card with a framed capture and framed buttons (`local-trash-listed-1440-light`). The brand deck's grammar asks for ruled rows and not boxes. NEXT.md 4.1.3 item 10 names `/decks` alone, so no row of the round covers the trash page. It is recorded for the owner of the pages.

P2-3. **A store test pins the seed deck at 85 slides, so check step 5 reads red while the chain's import is in the tree (B4, severity 1).** `packages/store/src/templates.test.ts` 269 (from `c17fd8bf`) expects `decks/gt-brand/slides` to hold 85 files. Check step 7 imports the live Prototemplate deck into that folder in place, with 95 slides, and nothing puts it back. A full chain runs step 5 before step 7 and passes. Step 5 on any later run in the same tree reads `expected 95 to be 85` (11:24Z, this pass). The test can read the template's own record, or the chain can import into a scratch folder.

# Round 1, the production table (2026-10-05)

The ship of Round 1 (`docs/NEXT.md` 4.1 and the hotfixes H2 to H10 of 3.2) to `main` and the verifier's reading of it on production. The ship step's record, the verifier's notes and the guard's log (`~/.config/turboslide/gt-follow.log`) are the sources, and the ship notes step wrote this section on 2026-10-05. Every time is UTC on 2026-10-05. Every load is the one minute load of the shared machine, which other sessions' jobs kept between 24 and 95 during Round 1's ship. The ledgers, the guard's files of each pass and the pictures named here are under `docs/gslides-parity/round1/verification/production/`: each JSON file under 81 KB, with share link paths written as `/s/<link>`, and 9 WebP pictures of 274,074 B in all.

## R1P.1 Verdict

Round 1 is on production. The seven groups went out in order from the branch `round1/ship` (worktree `/Users/kevinliu/repos/Turboslide-next`, head `a3ca96da`), and the guard deployed each one with no hold, no error line and no rollback. Each pass read the hosted smoke 42/42 on the preview and on production, and each read the production realtime rows green, three of them after one rerun of a timing row.

The ship step read every row each group entered or changed on production with that group's own spec. No row of the round read red twice for a reason of its push. `chrome.buttons.one-rule` read red twice after group 4 on two 8 px corners that production carried before that push, and group 5's `19b964bd` squared them; the row passed after group 5.

The verifier then read Round 1 on production at `a3ca96da` from 11:40Z to 13:15Z. Every row of the round that production can drive passed: 51 spec rows in one run and the 22 walk rows of the round in one walk of 10 areas. The hand drives found no broken feature of Round 1. They found four severity 2 defects and eight severity 1 defects a seller meets, and the eight earlier rounds' rows that read red in the ship's walks read red again (R1P.5). Lanes A to D of the Round 1 follow-up fix them on the branch `followup/round`, which no push has carried yet.

A scan of the added lines of `origin/main..a3ca96da` for key, token and private key patterns found nothing. Production `/decks` at `4aa32718` listed every deck of the store to an anonymous visitor.

# Landing, the production table (2026-10-05)

The ship of the landing redesign (`docs/LANDING.md`) from the branch `landing/ship` to `main`, its holds, the fix forward of its stylesheets, and the verifier's reading of it on production. The ship records, the fix record, the verifier's notes, the people band's record and the guard's log are the sources, and the ship notes step wrote this section on 2026-10-05. Every time is UTC on 2026-10-05. Every load is the one minute load of the shared machine, which other sessions' jobs held between 25 and 560 from 13:00Z to 23:45Z. The ledgers, the guard's files of each pass and the pictures named here are under `docs/gslides-parity/landing/verification/production/`: each JSON file under 25 KB, with share link paths written as `/s/<link>`, and 16 WebP pictures of 464,326 B in all.

## LP.1 Verdict

Landing 1 to 4 are on production. `https://www.turboslide.com` serves `617117e85e5c646977ade9a20df7c2a28591f6df` since 20:30:24Z (`turboslide-cr9ph0wys`), and the production Worker's `/health` answers the same commit.

The ship took six pushes and eleven guard passes. The first push of landing 1 (`a21c7358`) was a regression: its unscoped `.ts-row` rules broke `/decks`'s list rows, the guard held it on the preview, and production never served it. The fix forward scoped every landing selector that named a class used outside the landing and pinned the rule with a unit test. Every later hold was a production realtime row: a network outage of this machine (landing 2), a colo check that reads the room's latest request (landing 3), and timing bounds missed at loads over 100 with nothing lost (landing 4 and the people band). Each of the first three cleared on a retry and deployed.

The verifier's reading on production at `617117e8` found no severity 3 or severity 2 defect and six severity 1 defects (LP.6). Every band draws and answers its controls at 1440 and 390 in both appearances, and no landing stylesheet reaches `/decks`, the editor, its dialogs or Version history.

The two people band `0d75ab90` is on `main` and not on production. The guard held it three times on production realtime timing rows, and both retries the ship rule allows for a sha were used. It is the one severity 3 finding of the ship. Production serves `617117e8`, `gt-follow.held` holds `0d75ab90` and `gt-follow.last` holds `617117e8`.

# Round 1 follow-up, the production table (2026-10-06)

The ship of the Round 1 follow-up from the branch `followup/round` to `main`, its two holds and their retries, the ship step's readings and the verifier's reading on production. The ship record, the verifier's notes, the lanes' and the integrator's notes and the guard's log (`~/.config/turboslide/gt-follow.log`) are the sources, and the ship notes step wrote this section on 2026-10-06. Every time is UTC on 2026-10-06. Every load is the one minute load of the shared machine (18 cores), which other sessions' jobs kept between 36 and 308 from 06:58Z to 12:30Z. The ledgers, the guard's files of each pass and the pictures named here are under `docs/gslides-parity/round1/verification/followup/`: 34 JSON files, each under 68 KB and none holding a share link, and 10 WebP pictures of 203,968 B in all.

## R1F.1 Verdict

The Round 1 follow-up is on production. `https://www.turboslide.com` serves `95808c3624343e44b4c748ece84fda7d2460a36a` since 11:18:59Z (`turboslide-3rbzs16vj`), and the production Worker's `/health` answers the same commit with realtime on and the app origin `https://www.turboslide.com`. GET `/api/agent` answered that commit at 11:25Z, 11:29:21Z and 12:21:56Z.

The five groups went out in the integrator's order C, B, A, D, E from `followup/round` (worktree `/Users/kevinliu/repos/Turboslide-next`, head `95808c36`), seven guard passes in all. The guard held C and D once each on walk rows of the version history that a refused write of the walk deck had broken, at loads of 67 and 214. On each held preview the red rows passed two narrowed readings, and neither group changes the code those rows drive, so each hold was cleared with its sha's first retry. Every deployed pass read the hosted smoke 42/42 on the preview and on production and the three production realtime rows 3 of 3 at the first reading. No pass rolled production back.

The ship step read every row each group entered or changed on production with that group's own spec, and the verifier then read every item of the five lanes by hand on production at 1440 and 390 in both appearances. No regression of the follow-up and no severity 3 defect was found. Two standing severity 2 rows remain, both moved to Round 2 by the integrator, and the verifier found four severity 1 defects outside the follow-up's items (R1F.6). No word a person reads names the competitor on any page or dialog the verifier drove.

With each tile picture answered 1 s late by a route in the browser, the tiles in view drew within 2 s, and 69 requests of tiles the scroll passed were ended (`net::ERR_ABORTED`).

Observations, none a defect of the follow-up:

1. The refused page at `/edit/Not_A_Slug` and `/deck/Not_A_Slug` answers HTTP 500. A 404 needs `notFound()` from the edit and deck loaders, which lane C did not change.
2. Lane B's rotation rests on PowerPoint turning a graphic frame that carries `rot`. The file carries it. Quick Look does not turn it (it draws the table as two long upright rules and no chart), Keynote was not read (an AppleEvent timeout at the open) and PowerPoint is not on this machine.
3. The Import example lists 85 slides (the deck `gt-brand`), and the template gallery lists the General Translation brand deck at 95 slides (the template `gt-brand`).
4. Two `docs/gslides-parity/` path citations stay in CSS comments that ship in every page's HTML and in the Web page download (`packages/render/src/block-css.ts` 273, `packages/theme/src/gt-ink-paper/sheet.css` 892). The guard allows the planning folder's paths.
5. At 390 in the phone context the filmstrip card and the selected object carry another browser's presence label when the 1440 browser of the same run has the deck open. That is presence.

The design round's verifier, pass 1, on `design/round` at `d863c179` (the integrator's note over `3e3654b8`), in `/Users/kevinliu/repos/Turboslide-design`, from 04:40 to 05:20 PDT on 2026-10-06. One node-server build of the head (34 s at a load of 43) was served on port 4671 with the round's environment (a tmp store, the memory tier, secrets made per start and never printed) under `.turboslide/build.lock`; port 4681 was not used. Every drive ran in Playwright's Chromium with its scrollbars shown, at human speed (a pause of 20 to 40 ms between steps, typing at 50 to 70 ms a key), at 1440 by 900 and 390 by 844 in both appearances, with WebKit and Firefox where named. ## DR-V1.1 Verdict

Not ready to ship. Kevin's first and fifth items are done on the editor and the landing and hold by hand. His second item is not on this branch. His third and fourth items are done in part. The head names Google Slides on `/home` and in the README, which production no longer does.

| Kevin's item | Reading on the head |
| --- | --- |
| 1. Layering and corners | Done. The presence tooltip paints over the name prompt bar with its own ring, the bar sits 4 px under the title row, menus, submenus, context menus, pickers, tooltips, toasts and the bar draw 6 px with the `--pt-edge` frame and the ring, dialogs 8 px, Slideshow and Share one 6 px corner, "Link copied" over the Share dialog. Left: the Theme panel's 12 fields and selects are square (finding 5) |
| 2. Themes | Not done. D3's four pushes are on `d3/stage` and wait for the seam. A new deck and Blank are `gt-ink-paper`; the Theme panel lists no theme; Reset reads "Reset to General Translation" and draws the GT mark, rails, rules, crosses and counter on a Blank deck (finding 1) |
| 3. The landing | Done for the navigation, the hero, the numbers row, menus, canvas, Tailor, the two diagrams, features and the close. Not done: themes and kits, agents, two people, the captured presenter view, the Download dialog and the pattern stills (finding 3) |
| 4. Colour and the face | The colour tokens come from colorjs.io and are current; the face is Inter 4.001 by Rasmus Andersson in one Latin subset; no Berkeley Mono anywhere. Left: GT's alternates on `/decks`, the trash and the book view, and "GT Inter Display" in the PowerPoint file (finding 4) |
| 5. Scrollbars | Done in Chromium and WebKit: every scroller draws the 8 px gutter or none. Firefox's gate applies; its drawn bar could not be seen under Playwright (section DR-V1.7) |
| "dont mention google slides at all" | Broken on the head: `/home`'s description and footer, README line 8, the CLI help and the generated contracts. Main's guard reads 2,294 mentions in 61 files on this tree. The seam brings main's removal and its guard (finding 2) |

Each drive made its own deck on 4671 and removed it through `deck.trash` and `deck.remove` as its owner (every removal answered 404 after).

**Layering (Kevin's screenshot 1).** Chromium at 1440 and 390 in both appearances, Firefox at 1440 light:

- With the name prompt bar open: the title row ends at y 44; the bar is 1033,48 to 1428,76, 6 px, `1px solid --pt-edge`, the ring, in the `bar` layer and the top layer. The presence tooltip ("Collaborators. Who is in this presentation now. Nobody else has it open") is 751,44 to 1031,115 in the `tooltip` layer; the two plates share no edge. Moved onto the bar, the tooltip paints over it with its ring unbroken (`layers-bar-tooltip-1440-dark.jpg`, `layers-tooltip-over-bar-1440-dark.jpg`). `elementsFromPoint` does not list the tooltip, which takes no pointer events, so the picture is the reading. At 390 the bar is 63,88 to 378,116, 4 px under the toolbar row, over the top edge of the slide (`layers-context-390-dark.jpg`).
- File 8,73 to 229,446 and its submenu 224,186 to 444,252, both 6 px in the `popover` layer. With Insert open, the toolbar Undo tooltip (71,114 to 177,147) is drawn over the menu's left edge (`layers-menu-tooltip-1440-light.jpg`).

- Share opens an 8 px window in the `dialog` layer and the name prompt bar closes. Copy link shows "Link copied" at 8,852 to 139,892, 6 px, `toast` layer; at its centre the stack is the snackbar's text, the snackbar, then the dialog's scrim (`layers-share-snackbar-1440-dark.jpg`). Share's access control is the browser's select, so no chrome menu opens from a dialog.

The Background dialog draws its colours inline (`pickers-bg-dialog-plate-1440-light.jpg`).

Visible words outside slides, code and terminals: 1,964 on the head (1,814 without the two people band) and 1,858 on production. The page's description reads "Turboslide is a slides editor in the browser. It has Google Slides' menus and shortcuts. No account is needed."; production's names no other product. No `img` on `/home` failed to load and no request answered 400 or more in the four runs; the hidden appearance's twins were not requested.

**Scrollbars.** Chromium and WebKit, every scroller on `/home`, `/decks`, the templates gallery, the editor, the Insert menu, Keyboard shortcuts and `/deck/gt-brand`: a gutter of 8 px or none, the document's 8 px. The filmstrip draws a 4 px thumb with round ends in its 8 px gutter in WebKit, and the font list in Chromium (`scroll-filmstrip-webkit-light.jpg`, `scroll-fontlist-chromium-light.jpg`, `scroll-home-webkit-light.jpg`). Firefox answers false to `selector(::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb)` and reads `scrollbar-color: rgba(7, 7, 7, 0.44) transparent` on the root.

**The regression set.**

- Drag, resize and rotate, Chromium 1440 light, each on its own slide: a rectangle (a press and drag on its body 880,135 to 738,220; the south east handle 320x162 to 405x205; the rotation handle 20), a text box (typed "Typed by the verifier"; press and drag 880,135 to 738,220; after Escape the move handle, eight resize handles and the rotation handle), a 3 by 2 table (320,218 to 180,303; 960 to 1045 wide; 20), a column chart (320,218 to 137,317; 960x540 to 1079x599; 20), an uploaded picture (361,395 to 235,466; 320x200 to 405x243; 20) and a line (992,108 to 864,178) (`drag2-rectangle-pressdrag.jpg`, `drag2-text-box-selected.jpg`, `objects-picture-light.jpg`, `objects-chart-light.jpg`). A click on a selected box that holds text starts typing in it and hides the handles; Escape returns the box with its handles.
- File > Download > PDF Document: `Export light.pdf`, 16,207 B, `%PDF-1.4`, 2 pages. Download options with PowerPoint: `Export light.pptx`, 36,752 B, a zip whose slides name "GT Inter Display" in 5 runs.
- Slideshow: the show in the `show` layer and the top layer at 1440 by 900; the presenter view at `/present/<id>` with its timer and "2 of 2" in tabular figures (`export-presenter-light.jpg`).
- Share: Copy link writes "Link copied".
- Sign in: the dialog opens from the editor and from `/decks`. The editor's body scrolls by 35 px (250 over 215) and draws a thumb over an empty band in both appearances; the pages' dialog does not (`signin-editor-light.jpg`, `walk-signin-editor-1440-dark.jpg`, `walk-signin-pages-1440-light.jpg`).
- `/decks` lists the deck with its 6 px search pill, view switch and sort select (`walk-decks-1440-dark.jpg`); the trash and the refused page draw 6 px buttons (`walk-trash-1440-light.jpg`, `walk-refused-1440-dark.jpg`).

Loads during the drives: 43 to 137.

## DR-V1.7 Not driven

- Firefox's drawn bar: Playwright's Firefox computes `scrollbar-width: none` on every element of any page (a blank page reads the same), so the `* { scrollbar-width: thin }` rule cannot be seen there.
- Layering in WebKit (the cookie note in DR-V1.4).
- `home.budget.frame`, `home.budget.main-thread` and the timing clause of `home.motion.pause`: not read at a load of 38.6.
- The hover preview and the show's Keyboard shortcuts card were not opened by these drives; `chrome.layers.top-layer` and `present.presenter.surfaces` passed in run 1.
- The plate menus of a signed in person (account, roster) by hand; `chrome.radius.floating` passed in run 1.

## DR-V1.8 For Kevin

1. The landing's own script reads 119,854 B gzip against its 120,000 B line. The captured presenter view, the Download dialog and the 17 pattern stills need about 2,000 B more: either the line rises to 124,000 B or a band's chunk is cut first.
2. The order the ship needs: the seam (origin/main into `design/round`, which removes the Google Slides words and brings the guard and the Sign in height), then D3's port with DR-D3#1 shipped before DR-D3#2, then DR-D2#2b, DR-D4#1b, #5, #6 and DR-D5#1b to #3b, then DR-D1#4 and DR-D1#5.

# Design round, pass 2 (2026-10-05)

The design round's verifier, pass 2, after the fix round of pass 1, on `design/round` at `78806ee2` (the integrator's note after the fix round, tree `999e4b43`), in `/Users/kevinliu/repos/Turboslide-design`, from 08:58 to 09:40 PDT on 2026-10-06. Read first: `docs/DESIGN.md`, `docs/gslides-parity/design-round/integrator.md` (both sections) and the fix round sections of `d2.md`, `d3.md`, `d4.md` and `d5.md`. One node-server build of the head (`NITRO_PRESET=node-server vite build -c vite.deploy.config.ts`, exit 0 in 15 s at a load of 39 to 43, under `.turboslide/build.lock`) was served on port 4671 with the round's environment (a tmp store in `.turboslide/verify2-overlay`, the memory tier, the fake Google client pair of `apps/studio/playwright.config.ts`, secrets made per start and never printed); port 4681 was not used. Hand drives ran in Playwright's Chromium with its scrollbars shown, at a pause of 20 to 40 ms between steps and typing at 55 to 70 ms a key, at 1440 by 900 and 390 by 844 in both appearances, with WebKit and Firefox where named. Every drive made its own decks and removed each through `deck.trash` and `deck.remove` (every removal read 404 after). Production (`https://www.turboslide.com`) was read only: `/home` at 1440 dark with no write. Nothing was pushed or deployed and no setting changed. Loads are one minute load averages (25 to 45 during this pass); a timing at a load over 24 is not a verdict. The server on 4671 stopped at 09:27 and the build lock was released before this note was written. Pass 1's eight findings of severity 2 or 3 were measured first; the regression set was run once, lighter than pass 1.

## DR-V2.1 Verdict

Not ready to ship, for three reasons outside Kevin's five items as he wrote them: the unparkable row `decks.pages.pictures-load` is red on the head (the template cards name no theme, finding 1), origin/main's people band (`0a79db8e`, live on production) is not on `design/round` (finding 2), and DR-D1#5 has not landed. Each of Kevin's five items holds by hand on this build. Pass 1's findings 1 to 8 are fixed; finding 3 is fixed except DESIGN.md 8.8 and 8.9.

| Kevin's item | Reading on the head |
| --- | --- |
| 1. Layering and corners | Done, unchanged since pass 1. The presence tooltip (751,44 to 1031,115, `tooltip` layer) paints over the name prompt bar (1033,48 to 1428,76, `bar` layer) with its ring; File and its submenu, the context menu, the font picker, the text colour plate and the Theme panel's display font picker are 6 px `pt-float` plates in the `popover` layer; Share is an 8 px window in the `dialog` layer and closes the bar; "Link copied" is a 6 px plate in the `toast` layer over the dialog's scrim. No radius off the ladder and no square boxed control in 21 states at 1440 light and 18 at 390 dark, the Theme panel included |
| 2. Themes | Done. A new deck is Simple; the picker lists Simple, General Translation, Swiss, Mint, Coral, Night, Slate, Sand and Signal with Simple checked; Swiss stays after a reload, an agent's `slide.new` over HTTP, Import slides from a Simple deck, a kit change (the counter turned off) and "Reset to Swiss"; the Blank card makes Simple; the GT brand template makes General Translation. The 16 `themes.*` rows passed. Left: the template cards name no theme (finding 1) |
| 3. The landing | Done for the navigation, the hero, the numbers row, menus, canvas, Tailor, themes and kits (nine tiles), the captured presenter view, the Download dialog and the 17 pattern stills. 785 visible words at 1440 against production's 896 today; 27 pictures, none broken, no answer of 400 or more. Left: DESIGN.md 8.8 and 8.9 (finding 3) and the people band of origin/main (finding 2) |
| 4. Colour and the face | Done. One font file requested on `/home` (`InterVariable-latin`, 113,752 B); the build serves only `InterVariable-*` files; no element outside slides draws `cv11` or `ss01` on `/decks`, the trash, the gallery or `/deck/gt-brand`; the PowerPoint file names "Inter Display Medium" and no "GT" family; no text pair under its floor in the editor, the pages or the landing (the search card's chips pass); `Berkeley` appears in the tree only in sentences that say it is not used and in another site's research record, and nowhere in the build |
| 5. Scrollbars | Done in Chromium and WebKit: the filmstrip draws a 4 px thumb in an 8 px gutter in WebKit dark; `decks.pages.scrollbar` passed. Firefox reads the root's `scrollbar-color` (`rgba(242, 242, 240, 0.44) transparent` in dark); Playwright's Firefox computes `scrollbar-width: none` even for an inline `thin` on a blank page, so its drawn bar cannot be seen here |
| No word names the competitor | Done. Main's guard test (`packages/lint/src/brand/competitor.test.ts`) passes on the tree; `/home`'s title and description name no other product; the footer names General Translation only. The client build carries the competitor's name once, in the deck lint's list of proper nouns (`packages/lint/src/context.ts`, the words its sentence case rule leaves capitalised), which no person reads |

# Design round, pass 3 (2026-10-06)

The design round's verifier, pass 3, after the rebase onto origin/main `0a79db8e` and finishing round 1, on `design/round` at `78110596` (tree `6ddb8487`, 64 commits over origin/main, which is its ancestor), in `/Users/kevinliu/repos/Turboslide-design`, from 13:24 to 15:45 PDT on 2026-10-06. Read first: `docs/DESIGN.md`, "Design round, pass 2" above, `docs/gslides-parity/design-round/integrator.md` and the finishing round sections of `d2.md`, `d3.md`, `d4.md` and `d5.md`. One node-server build of the head (`NITRO_PRESET=node-server pnpm --filter @turboslide/studio build:deploy`, exit 0 in 305 s at a load of 251 to 140, under `.turboslide/build.lock`) was served twice with the round's environment (a tmp store, the memory tier, the fake Google client pair of `apps/studio/playwright.config.ts`, secrets made per start and never printed): on 4671 for the gate runs (overlay `.turboslide/verify3-overlay`) and on 4681 for the hand drives and the narrowed readings (overlay `.turboslide/verify3b-overlay`). Hand drives ran in Playwright's Chromium with its scrollbars shown, at a pause of 20 to 40 ms between steps and typing at 50 to 70 ms a key, at 1440 by 900 and 390 by 844 in both appearances, with WebKit where named. Every drive made its own decks and removed each through `deck.trash` and `deck.remove` (every removal read 404 after). Production (`https://www.turboslide.com`) was read on `/home` at 1440 dark and 390 light, and at 390 dark in the editor with one scratch deck of this pass (`untitled-20261006-v21v`), removed by its id with `scratchpad/realtime/remove-deck.mjs` (trash 200, remove 200, `deck.info` 404 after). Nothing was pushed or deployed and no setting changed. Other sessions' jobs held the one minute load at 120 to 650 through the pass (never stopped), so no timing reading here is a verdict. Both servers were stopped and the build lock released before this note was written.

## DR-V3.1 Verdict

Not ready to ship. Each of Kevin's five items holds by hand on this build and every finding of pass 2 is fixed or restated, but three defects of severity 2 stand:

1. `decks.home.pictures-three-widths`, a row of the unparkable `decks` feature, is red on the head in every reading: `/home` is 14,196 px tall at 1440 (line 14,000), 14,145 at 1280, 15,585 at 390 and 15,352 at 320 (line 15,000), in both appearances. Production reads 13,053 and 13,377.
2. The colour pickers draw General Translation's palette on the other eight themes: on a Mint, Swiss or Night deck the text colour plate's brand kit row reads Text `#070707`, Background `#ffffff` and Primary `#2f5ce0`, the Background dialog's Paper swatch is the chrome's paper, and Add to Theme with Paper writes `#ffffff` and turns every Mint slide white.
3. The hero reflows when Inter arrives after the first paint: at 390 the h1 sits on three lines in the fallback face and on two in Inter, so the lead, the buttons and the editor frame move up 44 px (CLS 0.0369 with the font answered after 3 s; production 0.0056), and at 1440 the lead goes from four lines to three (0.0028; production 0.0005). `home.page.bands-after-load` (unparkable) read it red in the run of record at both widths and in the second narrowed reading at 1440.

# Design round, pass 4 (2026-10-06)

The design round's verifier, pass 4, after the rebase onto origin/main `0a79db8e` and finishing round 2, on `design/round` at `b23c1e13` (tree `29820616`, 75 commits over origin/main, which is its ancestor and still the head of main), in `/Users/kevinliu/repos/Turboslide-design`, from 18:35 to 20:05 PDT on 2026-10-06. Read first: `docs/DESIGN.md`, "Design round, pass 2" and "Design round, pass 3" above, `docs/gslides-parity/design-round/integrator.md` and the finishing round 2 sections of `d3.md`, `d4.md`, `d5.md` and `requests.md`. One node-server build of the head (`NITRO_PRESET=node-server pnpm --filter @turboslide/studio build:deploy`, exit 0 in 113 s at a load of 206, under `.turboslide/build.lock`) was served twice with the round's environment (a tmp store, the memory tier, the fake Google client pair of `apps/studio/playwright.config.ts`, secrets made per start and never printed): on 4671 for the gate runs (overlay `.turboslide/verify4-overlay`) and on 4681 for the hand drives and a second stream of gate runs under its own lock (overlay `.turboslide/verify4b-overlay`). Hand drives ran in Playwright's Chromium with its scrollbars shown, at a pause of 25 to 40 ms between steps and typing at 50 to 70 ms a key, at 1440 by 900 and 390 by 844 in both appearances (1280 and 320 where named), with WebKit where named. Every drive made its own decks and removed each through `deck.trash` and `deck.remove` (every removal read 404 after). Production (`https://www.turboslide.com`) was read only, on `/home` at 1440 dark and 390 light, with no write. Nothing was pushed or deployed and no setting changed. Other sessions' jobs held the one minute load at 105 to 258 through the pass (never stopped), so no timing reading here is a verdict. Both servers were stopped by their own pids and the build lock released at 19:55, before this note was written.

## DR-V4.1 Verdict

Not ready to ship. Every finding of pass 3 is fixed or restated, and each of Kevin's five items holds by hand on this build, but four defects of severity 2 stand, three of them in finishing round 2's own work:

1. `decks.home.capture-plain`, a row of the unparkable `decks` feature, is red in all three readings: the kits band's new Colors row draws General Translation's Primary and Accent swatches in `rgb(47, 92, 224)`, the selection blue the row forbids at rest, at 1440 and 390 in both appearances. DESIGN.md 8.0 was restated for them in `53d9f378`; the row was not.
2. The Colors row's cells are narrower than a label and a six digit value in tabular figures: in the dark appearance at 1440 and 1280 the Captions swatch is drawn over the last digit of "Background #070707" at rest, and at 320 the same value runs past the window and reads "#0707" (`kits-colors-overrun-1440-320-dark.jpg`).
3. The swatch of the token `blue` is named "GT blue" on every theme: on a Mint deck the green `#11734f` swatch of the text colour plate and of the Background dialog carries the tooltip and label "GT blue" (`plate-mint-gtblue-1440-light.jpg`).
4. The PowerPoint file writes a shadow or a word art outline in the `blue` token as `#2F5CE0` on every theme: on a Mint deck the editor draws both in `#11734f` and the .pptx holds `2F5CE0` for both.

# Design round, pass 5 (2026-10-07)

The design round's verifier, pass 5, after finishing round 3, on `design/round` at `9696fdb5` (tree `ce649e66`, 88 commits over origin/main `0a79db8e`, which is its ancestor and still the head of main and of production), in `/Users/kevinliu/repos/Turboslide-design`, from 00:41 to 03:35 PDT on 2026-10-07. Read first: `docs/DESIGN.md`, "Design round, pass 2" and "Design round, pass 4" above, `docs/gslides-parity/design-round/integrator.md` and the finishing round 3 sections of `d1.md`, `d2.md`, `d3.md` and `d4.md`. One node-server build of the head (`NITRO_PRESET=node-server pnpm --filter @turboslide/studio build:deploy`, exit 0 in 122 s at a load of 504 to 477, under `.turboslide/build.lock`, which this pass held until both servers stopped) was served twice with the round's environment (a tmp store, the memory tier, the fake Google client pair of `apps/studio/playwright.config.ts`, secrets made per start and never printed): on 4671 for gate runs (overlay `.turboslide/verify5-overlay`) and on 4681 for the hand drives and a second stream of gate runs under their own locks (overlay `.turboslide/verify5b-overlay`). Hand drives ran in Playwright's Chromium with its scrollbars shown, at a pause of 25 to 40 ms between steps and typing at 45 to 70 ms a key, at 1440 by 900 and 390 by 844 in both appearances (1280 and 320 where named), with WebKit where named. Every drive made its own decks and removed each through `deck.trash` and `deck.remove` (every removal read 404 after). Production (`https://www.turboslide.com`) was read on `/home` at 1440 dark and 390 light, and one scratch deck was made there for the chart reading of DR-V5.4 (`untitled-20261007-vrgj`, a bar chart inserted, no export); the page's own removal answered 401 to an anonymous request, so it was removed by id with the bearer through `remove-deck.mjs` under `with-tokens.mjs` (trash 200, remove 200, `deck.info` 404 after). Nothing was pushed or deployed and no setting changed. Other sessions' jobs held the one minute load at 128 to 504 through the pass (never stopped), so no timing reading here is a verdict. Both servers were stopped by their own pids at 03:22 and the build lock released, before this note was written.

- Export and Present (`export.mjs`, dark and light): as the paragraph under DR-V5.3's ledger; Slideshow opens the show in the `show` layer at 1440 by 900; the presenter view at `/present/<id>` reads "2 of 2".
- Share: Copy Link wrote "Link copied" in the four layering drives; the 37 share rows of run B2 passed; the editor's Share dialog draws its own rows (`-home-css.test.ts` passed).
- Sign in (`signin.mjs`): the editor's dialog in both appearances, an 8 px window with no scroller, "Continue with Google" with its mark; `accounts.signin.one-dialog` passed.
- `/decks`, the trash, the gallery, `/deck/gt-brand`, Not found and the refused page drew in both widths in the walk; Import slides as above.

**Two standing reds outside the round** (`chart5.mjs`, `assistview.mjs`):

- `charts.export.pdf` (feature `charts`): the export spec's bar chart (North, South and West at 660 by 360) draws its category labels "Nor…", "Sou…" and "West" in the editor, so the PDF's text holds "Nor…" and the row's "North is in the PDF text" fails. Production draws the same three labels on a scratch deck (`chart-labels-prod-left-head-right.jpg`); the row was not driven on main while the chart's text printed as Type 3 glyphs. D3's request of finishing round 3.
- `assist.viewer.disabled` (feature `assist`): a view link lands on `/edit/<id>` in View only with no Assist control in the title row, since `title.assist` carries `when: 'write'` in `packages/chrome/src/menus/model.ts` (Round 1, B3a#7, on main); the row reads the panel's sentence after a press on that control and finds none. The round's diff does not touch the item, the title row's script or `Assist.tsx`.

# Design round, the production table (2026-10-08)

The ship of the design round from the branch `design/round` to `main`, the six holds of D2 and their retries, the guard's rules of 2026-10-07, the ship step's readings per push and the production verifier's reading. The two ship records of the session's scratchpad (`ship-now/the-design-round.md`, rebuilt from the guard's log after the first ship agent's record was lost in a reboot, and `ship-now/the-design-round-part-2.md`), the production verifier's note `docs/gslides-parity/design-round/production.md`, `docs/gslides-parity/design-round/integrator.md` and the guard's log (`~/.config/turboslide/gt-follow.log`) are the sources, and the ship notes step wrote this section on 2026-10-08 from 05:44Z. Every time is UTC, on 2026-10-07 up to midnight and on 2026-10-08 after it. Every load is the one minute load of the shared machine, which other sessions' jobs kept between 28 and 288 from the first push to the last deployed line and between 16 and 513 during the readings after it. The ledgers, the guard's files of each pass and the pictures named here are under `docs/gslides-parity/design-round/production/`: 81 JSON files, two text files and 12 JPEGs of 571,192 B, none holding a token or a share link.

## DRP.1 Verdict

The design round is on production. `https://www.turboslide.com` serves `f2d48868b478b33baa744c5ffd5c091a385b966f` since 02:57:35Z (`turboslide-ar7dwyv4g`), and the production Worker's `/health` answers the same commit with realtime on and the app origin `https://www.turboslide.com`. GET `/api/agent` with the bearer answered that commit at 02:57:56Z, 03:38Z and through the verifier's reading from 04:15Z to 05:50Z.

The six groups went out in the integrator's order D1, D2, D5, D3 reader, D3, D4 from `design/round` (worktree `/Users/kevinliu/repos/Turboslide-design`, head `f2d48868`, 90 commits over `0a79db8e`, each authored and committed by `kevin@generaltranslation.com`), twelve guard passes in all. D1, D5, D3 reader, D3 and D4 each deployed on their first pass. The guard held D2 six times: twice on walk rows that passed two narrowed readings each, and four times on production realtime rows red twice after the promote, each of which rolled production and the Worker back to `6973fa9f` 7 to 13 minutes after the production deploy. Every red of D2 was a timing bound missed at a load of 96 to 252 with nothing lost, a row that passed both narrowed readings, or the lost word of `realtime.title.two-typers` that production's own `6973fa9f` shows at the same load (DRP.3). D2 deployed on its seventh pass at 23:14:59Z, the first under the guard's second change of 2026-10-07. No push regressed production, and no deployed push was rolled back.

The production verifier read Kevin's five items by hand on every page at 1440 and 390 in both appearances and ran the round's 98 rows on production. Every item holds on every page read. The verdict of that reading is "not ready" on one finding of severity 2: the landing's show is cut at 1440 by 900 and 1280 by 800 (DRP.6 finding 1). The other five findings are of severity 1, and no finding is of severity 3. No word a person reads names the competitor on any page or dialog the verifier drove.

The guard's rules of 2026-10-07. `~/.config/turboslide/gt-follow.sh` changed twice that day, each time with its loop restarted (the copies `gt-follow.sh.before-rerun-any` and `gt-follow.sh.before-realtime-inconclusive` beside it hold the earlier versions):

1. From 18:49:41Z (pid 60105): any red walk row is read once more, narrowed to its areas, and any red spec row once more, narrowed to its ids; the sha is held only when a row is red twice. Before, only a red whose reason matched the timing pattern was read again, and every other red held at once (as the holds of 18:18Z and 18:49Z did). The code's note: "red twice is the rule; a loaded machine reddens a different row each pass".
2. From 22:41:06Z (pid 98696): `realtime.title.two-typers` joins the standing rows (`decks.file.open-list-search`, `fonts.table.takes-family`) until the fix of its lost word ships, since production's `6973fa9f` lost a word in 1 of 5 readings before any later push; its observed text is logged on every pass. The timing pattern also matches `toBeLessThanOrEqual` and "one object across both origins" (the colo assertion, which polish two fixes). Production realtime rows red twice on timing alone at a one minute load over 24 keep the deployment, and the log says "production realtime inconclusive: red on timing alone at load <n>, the deployment stays"; a failure of any other kind still rolls production back.

# Polish two, pass 1 (2026-10-07)

Polish two's verifier, pass 1, on `polish2/round` at `9fefefc1` (the integrator's notes over `aec2d38a`; outside `docs/` its tree equals `4df41226`'s, the integrator's build of record), in `/Users/kevinliu/repos/Turboslide-polish2`, from 19:17 to 22:25 PDT on 2026-10-07. Read first: `docs/POLISH-2.md`, `docs/gslides-parity/polish-two/integrator.md`, the four lane notes (`f.md`, `n.md`, `a.md`, `d.md`) and `requests.md`. One node-server build of the head (`NITRO_PRESET=node-server vite build -c vite.deploy.config.ts` in `apps/studio`, exit 0 in 138 s, 19:17 to 19:19, load 104, under `.turboslide/build.lock`, which this pass held until its servers stopped at 22:15) was served twice with the round's environment (a tmp store in its own overlay, the memory tier, `TURBOSLIDE_LOCAL_OPEN=1`, a sqlite identity database each, the auth rate limit off, the fake Google pair, secrets made per start and never printed): on 4731 with `TURBOSLIDE_MAIL=capture` and on 4741 with `TURBOSLIDE_MAIL=off`, production's mode. For two comparisons the design round's tree `f2d48868` was written into the session's scratchpad with `git archive`, installed with `pnpm install --offline --frozen-lockfile` and built the same way (exit 0 in 1,234 s at a load of 140 to 280); it was served on 4741 in place of the mail off server from 21:25, after every mail off reading below. Hand drives ran in Playwright's Chromium (chromium-1217) at 1440 by 900 and 390 by 844 in both appearances, and at 1280, 1024, 768 and 320 where named, typing at 45 ms a key. The Claude app restarted at 20:30 and closed the browser of this pass's first walk mid run; the pass resumed from its own readings and ran the walk again. Nothing was pushed or deployed, no setting changed, and production was not read; every deck of this pass lived in the local servers' tmp overlays. Other sessions' jobs held the one minute load at 16 to 450 (never stopped), so a timing here is a verdict only where its load is named under 20.

## P2-V1.1 Verdict

Not ready: two findings of severity 2 (P2-V1.4): two docs pages scroll sideways at phone width (lane D, this round's), and an empty table's column guides vanish on a dark deck, a red walk row that a ship would park `tables` on (standing on the design round's tree, not this round's change). Each of Kevin's five asks is otherwise done on this build, and the regression set holds.

| Kevin's ask | Reading on the head |
| --- | --- |
| 1. "are we using the correct rasmus inter? please do" | Done. Every page loads one font file, `/assets/InterVariable-latin-CVp3QPVX.woff2`, 113,752 B, sha256 `eed13049...`, equal to `packages/fonts/assets/InterVariable-latin.woff2` and its `fonts.json` record. Read with fontTools (`.turboslide/venv`): name 5 "Version 4.001;git-9221beed3", axes opsz 14 to 32 (default 14) and wght 100 to 900 (default 400), and 42 features across GSUB and GPOS (aalt, calt, case, ccmp, cpsp, cv01 to cv13, dlig, dnom, frac, kern, locl, mark, mkmk, numr, ordn, pnum, salt, sinf, ss01 to ss08, subs, sups, tnum, zero); the vendored full upright is 352,240 B with sha256 `693b77d4...a8e3`, the release's. On `/home`, `/decks`, `/decks/templates`, `/new`, `/signin`, `/device`, `/docs`, `/docs/agents`, `/docs/reference`, `/docs/editor`, `/docs/shortcuts` and `/docs/reference/deck` at 1440 and 390 in both appearances (48 loads): one font file each, 0 elements outside a slide with a stylistic set or a character variant, 0 numbers alone in proportional figures. The rule, on a scratch copy of the tree (`git archive HEAD` with its own `git init`): `css/chrome-alternates` in enforce mode exit 0 clean, then exit 1 with `'cv11'` injected in a chrome rule (`ToolButton.css`) and in a JSX `fontFeatureSettings` (`ThemeButton.tsx`), and with `var(--display-features)` outside `.ts-sheet` and `font-variant-alternates: stylistic(x)` in `home.css`; the same token under `.ts-sheet` and `'cv11', 'ss01'` in the General Translation theme's own sheet passed. The three face rows passed |
| 2. "remove the pause unpause button in top bar" | Done. At 1440, 1280, 1024, 768, 390 and 320 in both appearances the header holds no `[data-motion-toggle]`; the page holds two, the hero terminal's 28 px icon button and the footer's "Pause Motion" text button, 134 px wide in both states (`home-foot-paused-1440-dark.jpg`). ## P2-V2.1 Verdict

Ready: no finding of severity 2 or 3. Pass 1's two findings of severity 2 are fixed (the docs no longer scroll sideways at 320, 390 or 768 on any of the 35 pages, and an empty table's guides read on a dark deck under the light chrome: `tables.light-appearance-guides` and `tables.cells.empty-grid-guides` passed in run W), and so are its findings 3 to 6 within their scope. Six findings of severity 1 remain (P2-V2.4): long flags in the reference still break after their hyphens and the table of contents breaks long action names with a few letters on the next line (D), the pressed footer motion button draws its plate outside the column (N), a signed in visitor sees an empty Sign In slot after the hairline on `/home` (A, with N), `/home`'s Sign In still draws after hydration (A), and check step 31 is red on `/decks` and `/docs` (the integrator). Every row of section 6 that ran passed with zero retries; `home.motion.pause`'s timing clause, `home.budget.frame` and `home.budget.main-thread` were not read at any load of this pass (100 to 172), and the do tier rows need two origins (P2-V2.6).

# Polish two, the production table (2026-10-08)

The ship of polish two from the branch `polish2/round` to `main` in four groups, the guard's readings of each push, the ship step's readings per group and the production verifier's reading. The ship step's record in the session's scratchpad (`ship-now/polish-two.md`), the production verifier's note `docs/gslides-parity/polish-two/production.md`, `docs/gslides-parity/polish-two/integrator.md`, "Polish two, pass 2" above and the guard's log (`~/.config/turboslide/gt-follow.log`) are the sources, and the ship notes step wrote this section on 2026-10-08 from 12:52Z. Every time is UTC on 2026-10-08. Every load is the one minute load of the shared machine, which other sessions' jobs kept between 59 and 100 from the first push to the last deployed line and between 43 and 89 during the verifier's reading after it. The ledgers, the guard's files of each pass and the pictures named here are under `docs/gslides-parity/polish-two/production/`: 27 JSON files, 8 text files and 10 JPEGs of 110,559 B, none holding a token or a share link.

## P2P.1 Verdict

Polish two is on production. `https://www.turboslide.com` serves `6fd18a49b5edee56631600be695172ddb9d7e508` since 12:01:56Z (`turboslide-7wjsml50k`), and the production Worker's `/health` answers the same commit with realtime on and the app origin `https://www.turboslide.com`. GET `/api/agent` and the Worker's `/health` answered that commit before and after the verifier's reading from 12:17Z to 12:52Z.

The four groups went out in the integrator's order F, N, A, D from `polish2/round` (worktree `/Users/kevinliu/repos/Turboslide-polish2`, head `6fd18a49`, 41 commits over `a3f646d8`, each authored and committed by `kevin@generaltranslation.com`), four guard passes in all. Each group deployed on its first pass, with no hold, no retry and no rollback. Two passes met a red production reading of `realtime.title.two-typers` that missed its 500 ms bound with every word present (P2P.3), and one walk row was red once on a request of this machine timing out and passed the guard's rerun. No push regressed production.

The production verifier re-walked Kevin's five asks by hand at 1440 and 390 in both appearances, and at 1280, 1024, 768, 360, 359 and 320 where they apply, and ran the round's spec rows and the help area on production. Each of the five asks holds. Its verdict is "ready": no finding of severity 2 or 3. Of the round's 28 spec rows 27 passed and `home.motion.pause` was not read at the load; the help area's 7 rows passed. Pass 2's findings 1, 2, 3 and 5 still show on production at severity 1, and the regression walk found three defects older than polish two, each at severity 1 (P2P.6).

2.
